# Skills · G · part 19

873 entries, gear-diagnosis to gem-release. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **gear-diagnosis**: Gear fault diagnosis workflow using vibration analysis via the predictive-maintenance-mcp server. Use this skill when the user says "gear fault", "gear diagnosis", "gear mesh", "gearbox analysis", "gear vibration", "tooth damage", "gear defect", "diagnose gear", "gear mesh frequency", "sideband analysis", or wants to detect gear faults from vibration signals. ([LGDiMaggio/predictive-maintenance-mcp](https://github.com/LGDiMaggio/predictive-maintenance-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/gear-diagnosis) · ★117)
- **gear-fatigue**: Gear fatigue — bending fatigue (AGMA 2101/ISO 6336), contact (pitting) fatigue, allowable bending stress, allowable contact stress, life factors, material factors, lubrication correction, micropitting, scuffing, gear fatigue test methods. ([MP-AI-20/mechanical-engineering-skills](https://github.com/MP-AI-20/mechanical-engineering-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gear-fatigue) · ★13)
- **gear-fitting**: Dress a bare game character with an outfit and worn gear and prove it fits — fit a ghost-mannequin garment onto the base body, smooth it, skin it to the same skeleton, mount rigid gear (helmet, quiver, shield, held weapon) on its bones, hide covered body parts by bone. Then check with numbers and renders that no body shows through in any clip, the cloth rests naturally and held gear clears the body. Use whenever clothes, armour or gear go on a body. ([pablolok/claude-skills](https://github.com/pablolok/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/published/gamedev/gamedev-animation/skills/gear-fitting) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **gear-fix-up**: Fold corrections into the right branch commit, following the 'Développer avec l'IA' guide (§5.3). Usage: /gear-fix-up [status|fold|help]. With no argument: routes each pending change to the branch commit that owns it and creates the `git commit --fixup` commits. `fold` replays them into their targets (autosquash rebase on master) before opening the PR. `status` lists what is pending. `help` explains everything doable with fixups. Refuses to rewrite history once the PR is open. Use when I say 'fixup', 'replier', 'corriger un commit précédent', 'j'ai modifié un module déjà commité', 'autosquash… ([GearProductions/whisper](https://github.com/GearProductions/whisper/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gear-fix-up))

- **gec-policy-relevance-and-implications**: Use when articulating the real-world, policy, and practice implications of a Global Environmental Change (GEC) manuscript.
